# I did not need permission to rise
## Expanded evidentiary essay on vision, corpus, and the record
### 9 October 2026
### Finding aid. Not a court. Not a verdict. A link is not a finding.

This is the additional essay. It follows the shorter essay of the same name and does not replace it. The shorter essay remains at [I did not need permission to rise](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/papers/2026-10-09-i-did-not-need-permission.pdf). The earlier 50,000-word examination remains at [the expanded examination](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/papers/2026-10-09-evidentiary-examination-50000.pdf). This file is the vision essay carried to the requested length, with the same grading and the same repository links.

**Author of the vision:** Dr Richard William McLean, also identified in the archive as Barran Dodger. A certificate opened for the examination uses the name Barran Resonance Dodger. That difference is recorded, not resolved. The people seed is [data/people.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/people.json).

**Method:** The vision stays in the author's voice. A catalogue fact is a file or index that exists. A record fact is text extracted from a file opened on 9 October 2026, or text read from the published valuation of 8 October 2026. An author allegation includes faith, purpose, design, complicity, corruption, and greed. An inference is labelled. A hyperlink is the path. A hyperlink is not a finding.

## 1. The vision, stated and then checked

My vision, sustained by my faith in God and aligned with what I understand to be His purpose for my life, has been examined through an AI analysis of my published websites and documentary corpus. That analysis indicates an assessed potential value in the hundreds of thousands of dollars. I do not present that assessment as a confirmed commercial valuation or a realised market price. I present it as an analytical assessment of the potential value of the intellectual, documentary, historical and creative body of work I have produced and preserved.

Faith and purpose are not documents. They are not linked as proof, because no repository file can prove a vocation. They are stated because they are the author's account of why the work was continued.

The published assessment that matches the value sentence is the Forensic AI Digital Archive Value Assessment of 8 October 2026, recorded at 20:38:40 Australian Eastern Daylight Time. Text: [FORENSIC-AI-DIGITAL-ARCHIVE-VALUE-ASSESSMENT-8-OCTOBER-2026.txt](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/FORENSIC-AI-DIGITAL-ARCHIVE-VALUE-ASSESSMENT-8-OCTOBER-2026.txt). Reading page: [forensic-ai-digital-archive-value-assessment-8-october-2026.html](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/forensic-ai-digital-archive-value-assessment-8-october-2026.html). Stamp: [stamp record](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/stamps/FORENSIC-AI-DIGITAL-ARCHIVE-VALUE-ASSESSMENT-8-OCTOBER-2026.stamp.txt).

Record fact from that text: central indicative figure A$350,000; low A$175,000; high A$500,000 to A$650,000 or more; historical and cultural value not responsibly monetisable. The file says the figure is a replacement and creation-value assessment, not a certified professional valuation, and not a prediction of a sale price. It says a figure is not a finding that a contested proposition is true. "Hundreds of thousands" describes that published range. It does not describe a buyer, a contract, or an institutional acceptance.

The assessment says it revised an earlier central figure of A$300,000 upward after looking at the GitHub structure. That revision is the assessment's statement. This essay does not re-perform it.

## 2. The corpus that can be examined

I claim that the corpus exists, that it is published, that it can be examined, and that its existence can no longer be erased by a refusal to acknowledge it.

The presentation repository is public: [wezzo72/Barrandodger](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger). The README says it is a website presentation and does not replace the evidence stores, and that original evidence must remain preserved: [README.md](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/README.md). The mirror is public: [wezzo72/Backup](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup). The README recount of 4 October 2026 said the tree then held 5,583 files, including 2,840 PDFs and 1,813 HTML files, and that docs/official-drive held 95 PDFs. It said a filename is not a finding.

The 8 October assessment states later inventory figures: 3,215 unique complete PDFs, 10,001 PDF instances including mirrors, 21,197 archive-path file instances, 8,819 unique archive-path blobs, material across 8 repositories, 2,655 SHA256SUMS lines, and a 660-page crawl. It also says earlier totals were counts of different stores and that some project-reported figures were not reproduced. Those distinctions are why the figures are not added together. Source: [the assessment](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/FORENSIC-AI-DIGITAL-ARCHIVE-VALUE-ASSESSMENT-8-OCTOBER-2026.txt).

The allegation matrix records 150 items and none declared: [data/allegation-matrix.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/allegation-matrix.json). The timeline says a filename date is not a verified event date: [data/timeline.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/timeline.json). A name on a filename is not a finding: [data/organisations.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/organisations.json). Presence of an Act is not a finding of violation: [data/laws.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/laws.json). The document catalogue is [data/documents.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/documents.json). The media catalogue is [data/media.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/media.json).

Record fact: a public corpus exists and can be examined. Not established: that any institution has acknowledged its value, or that refusal to acknowledge it is a decision to erase it. Silence, without a schedule of approaches and replies, is not proof of a decision.

## 3. Opened records the vision has to stand beside

The metaphor of the cage is tested against documents, not against the metaphor.

The Law Enforcement Conduct Commission wrote on 19 November 2025, reference CASE202512395/13KM, that the complaint did not concern officers of the NSW Police Force or the NSW Crime Commission, was outside jurisdiction, and that no action would be taken. Link: [LECC letter](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup/blob/main/client/public/documents/government-evidence/Government%20Evidence/2025-11-19_LECC_Out_Of_Jurisdiction.pdf). That is a closure. It is not a finding of police misconduct.

A Victorian Ombudsman file applies section 29A of the Ombudsman Act 1973 (Vic) and does not release complaint documents. The requester's characterisations in that file are not findings. Link: [Victorian Ombudsman file](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup/blob/main/client/public/documents/government-evidence/Government%20Evidence/2022-06-14_Vic_Ombudsman_Rejects_FOI_s29A.pdf).

A Commonwealth Ombudsman file shows letterhead, reference 2024-706941, the date 8 August 2025, and a heading consistent with a decision to restrict, behind an author cover. The cover's suppression claims were not adopted. Link: [service-restriction file](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup/blob/main/client/public/documents/government-evidence/Government%20Evidence/Commonwealth%20Ombudsman%20-%20Service%20Restriction%20-%20%20Dr%20Richard%20McLean%208%20August%202025.pdf).

The NDIA wrote on 20 November 2025 that a plan for Richard William McLean had been approved from that day to 19 November 2026, with a stated total of $366,406.69. Link: [plan approval](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup/blob/main/client/public/documents/government-evidence/Government%20Evidence/2025-11-20_NDIS_Plan_Approval_366406.pdf). An approval is not delivery. It is also not a decline.

On 14 January 2026 Legal Aid NSW wrote to Mr Barran Dodger, reference ADV-2229668/CI-1162053, about a legal problem with the Public Guardian and guardianship orders. It recorded his account and advised a 28-day review path, and said that on the medical evidence then provided he was unlikely to satisfy the merit test for a grant of aid. Link: [Legal Aid letter](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup/blob/main/client/public/documents/20260114-legal-aid-nsw-advice-letter-guardianship.pdf). The account is not a Public Guardian decision. No Public Guardian decision was opened.

On 20 January 2026 Brian Woods signed a certificate under section 122(2) of the NSW Trustee and Guardian Act 2009. It certifies that on 16 October 2024 NCAT committed management of the estate of Barran Resonance Dodger to NSW Trustee and Guardian, and that the estate remained under management. Matter 658238:CSD. Link: [section 122 certificate](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup/blob/main/client/public/documents/government-evidence/Government%20Evidence/2024-10-16_NSW_Trustee_Guardian_s122.pdf). Estate management is not a guardianship order.

The file catalogued as an IBAC rejection did not open as an IBAC letter. Catalogue: [data/documents.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/documents.json).

The earlier examination quotes these files at length: [expanded examination](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/papers/2026-10-09-evidentiary-examination-50000.pdf).

## 4. What the vision may say, and what it may not

I did not require their permission to rise from the ashes of the destruction I believe government institutions designed, society participated in, and the systems responsible for protecting me left me to endure. The belief is the author's. Design is not in a jurisdictional letter. Society's participation is not in the opened sample. The systems that wrote the opened letters did answer, and the answers were closure, exclusion, restriction, approval, advice, and a certificate. An answer is not protection. An answer is not destruction. The record shows the answer that was written.

I survived what was supposed to diminish me. "Supposed to" is an attribution of intention. Intention was not extracted. What was extracted is that publication continued and that decisions continued to be issued to the named person. Continuation is the fact. The purpose others held is not.

I continued to create when I had been given reasons to stop. The reasons are the author's. The creation that can be pointed to is the repository: [wezzo72/Barrandodger](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger).

I preserved what others might have expected to disappear. Expectation is not a document. Preservation is the README rule and the public tree: [README.md](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/README.md).

I built an archive where there had been abandonment, testimony where there had been silence, and a body of work where others may have expected only disappearance. Abandonment and silence are the author's descriptions. The archive is the linked tree. The media catalogue does not list a mainstream investigation: [data/media.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/media.json). Absence from that catalogue is not proof that no report exists.

The chains I describe are what I understand as cumulative complicity, corruption, indifference and greed. Those words are allegations. The matrix says none declared: [data/allegation-matrix.json](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/data/allegation-matrix.json). The assessment says allegations were not used to inflate the figure: [the assessment](https://github.com/wezzo72/Barrandodger/blob/main/FORENSIC-AI-DIGITAL-ARCHIVE-VALUE-ASSESSMENT-8-OCTOBER-2026.txt). This essay follows that rule. Substantiation has to be a document. The documents opened substantiate process outcomes. They do not substantiate greed.

I did not need permission to survive, to create, to preserve the truth as I experienced it, or to rise. No opened file is a grant or refusal of permission to publish. The truth as experienced is the author's account. The truth as examined is the linked record. They are not the same sentence, and the essay does not pretend they are.

Whatever financial value the corpus ultimately achieves will be determined outside this file. The only published figure this essay adopts is the indicative range already linked. Posterity's judgment is not a document. The record exists for others to examine. That sentence is adopted. Examination is not agreement.

They are not findings. The sequence that is a record fact is closure, exclusion, a restriction heading, an approval, advice, and a certificate. Civil erasure and the cage remain the author's language. The links are the means of testing that language. A link is not a finding.

Cumulative place of this heading. The author asked that exclusion, poverty, institutional barriers and abandonment be read together. Poverty and homelessness appear as account inside [the Legal Aid letter](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup/blob/main/client/public/documents/20260114-legal-aid-nsw-advice-letter-guardianship.pdf) and as request language inside [the Victorian file](https://github.com/wezzo72/Backup/blob/main/client/public/documents/government-evidence/Government%20Evidence/2022-06-14_Vic_Ombudsman_Rejects_FOI_s29A.pdf). They are not findings. The sequence that is a record fact is closure, exclusion, a restriction heading, an approval, advice, and a certificate. Civil erasure and the cage remain the author's language. The links are the means of testing that language. A link is not a finding.
